import { describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import { normalizeScannedPackageClassification } from "../scanClassification";

function normalize(overrides: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return normalizeScannedPackageClassification({
    pktNr: "TEST",
    profil: null,
    kvalitet: null,
    anteckning: null,
    nomTjocklek: 47,
    nomBredd: 175,
    aktTjocklek: 45,
    aktBredd: 170,
    ...overrides,
  });
}

describe("normalizeScannedPackageClassification", () => {
  test("normaliserar C24 REGEL 4 RH", () => {
    const result = normalize({ profil: "C24 REGEL 4 RH" });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ profil: "Regel", kvalitet: "C24", anteckning: "4 RH" });
  });

  test("normaliserar C24 REGEL 4/4 RH och kompletterar befintlig anteckning", () => {
    const result = normalize({ profil: "C24 REGEL 4/4 RH", anteckning: "Halvpkt, Täckt" });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      profil: "Regel",
      kvalitet: "C24",
      anteckning: "Halvpkt, Täckt, 4 RH",
    });
  });

  test("normaliserar REGEL C24", () => {
    const result = normalize({ profil: "REGEL C24" });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ profil: "Regel", kvalitet: "C24", anteckning: null });
  });

  test("normaliserar felplacerad C24 med färdigt regelformat 45x170", () => {
    const result = normalize({ profil: "C24" });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ profil: "Regel", kvalitet: "C24", anteckning: null });
  });

  test("normaliserar 4/4 RH i kvalitet utan att ersätta C24", () => {
    const result = normalize({ profil: "C24", kvalitet: "4/4 RH", anteckning: "Täckt, 4/4RH" });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ profil: "Regel", kvalitet: "C24", anteckning: "Täckt, 4 RH" });
  });

  test.each([
    ["KD", 47, 45],
    ["4", 47, 45],
    ["C24", 65, 60],
    ["C24", 75, 70],
  ])("lämnar osäkert C24/%s med nom %i och akt %i orört", (kvalitet, nomTjocklek, aktTjocklek) => {
    const result = normalize({ profil: "C24", kvalitet, nomTjocklek, aktTjocklek });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ profil: "C24", kvalitet });
  });

  test("lämnar Sågat/C24 separat", () => {
    const result = normalize({
      profil: "Sågat",
      kvalitet: "C24",
      aktTjocklek: null,
      aktBredd: null,
    });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ profil: "Sågat", kvalitet: "C24", anteckning: null });
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/corsPolicy.test.ts ================

import express from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import type { Server } from "node:http";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import {
  getTrustedBrowserOrigins,
  trustedCorsOptions,
  trustedOriginGuard,
} from "./corsPolicy";

const productionOrigin = "https://lager.example.com";
const trustedOrigins = getTrustedBrowserOrigins({
  NODE_ENV: "production",
  TRUSTED_ORIGINS: productionOrigin,
});

let server: Server;
let baseUrl: string;

beforeAll(async () => {
  const app = express();
  app.use(trustedOriginGuard(trustedOrigins));
  app.use(cors(trustedCorsOptions(trustedOrigins)));
  app.get("/api/status", (_req, res) => res.json({ ok: true }));
  app.post("/api/extern/paket", (_req, res) => res.json({ accepted: true }));
  server = app.listen(0);
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.once("listening", resolve));
  const address = server.address();
  if (!address || typeof address === "string") throw new Error("Testservern startade inte");
  baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`;
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) =>
    server.close((error) => (error ? reject(error) : resolve())),
  );
});

describe("trusted browser origins", () => {
  test("loopback är endast betrodd i utvecklingsläge", () => {
    expect(getTrustedBrowserOrigins({ NODE_ENV: "development" })).toContain(
      "http://127.0.0.1",
    );
    expect(getTrustedBrowserOrigins({ NODE_ENV: "production" })).not.toContain(
      "http://127.0.0.1",
    );
  });

  test("tillåter exakt konfigurerad produktionsorigin med credentials", async () => {
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/status`, {
      headers: { Origin: productionOrigin },
    });

    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin")).toBe(productionOrigin);
    expect(response.headers.get("access-control-allow-credentials")).toBe("true");
  });

  test("nekar okänd origin", async () => {
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/status`, {
      headers: { Origin: "https://attacker.example" },
    });

    expect(response.status).toBe(403);
    expect(response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin")).toBeNull();
  });

  test("besvarar preflight för betrodd origin", async () => {
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/status`, {
      method: "OPTIONS",
      headers: {
        Origin: productionOrigin,
        "Access-Control-Request-Method": "GET",
      },
    });

    expect(response.status).toBe(204);
    expect(response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin")).toBe(productionOrigin);
    expect(response.headers.get("access-control-allow-methods")).toContain("GET");
  });

  test("släpper igenom requests utan Origin utan CORS-header", async () => {
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/status`);

    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin")).toBeNull();
  });

  test("Paketlapp-liknande serveranrop utan Origin fortsätter fungera", async () => {
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/extern/paket`, { method: "POST" });

    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await response.json()).toEqual({ accepted: true });
    expect(response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin")).toBeNull();
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/corsPolicy.ts ================

import type { RequestHandler } from "express";
import type { CorsOptions } from "cors";

function addExactOrigin(origins: Set<string>, value: string): void {
  const candidate = value.trim();
  if (!candidate) return;
  try {
    const url = new URL(candidate);
    if (
      (url.protocol !== "https:" && url.protocol !== "http:") ||
      url.username ||
      url.password ||
      url.search ||
      url.hash ||
      (url.pathname !== "/" && url.pathname !== "")
    ) {
      return;
    }
    origins.add(url.origin);
  } catch {
    // Invalid entries fail closed and are not trusted.
  }
}

export function getTrustedBrowserOrigins(
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
): ReadonlySet<string> {
  const origins = new Set<string>();

  for (const value of (env.TRUSTED_ORIGINS ?? "").split(",")) {
    addExactOrigin(origins, value);
  }
  for (const domain of (env.REPLIT_DOMAINS ?? "").split(",")) {
    const trimmed = domain.trim();
    if (trimmed) addExactOrigin(origins, `https://${trimmed}`);
  }
  if (env.REPLIT_DEV_DOMAIN?.trim()) {
    addExactOrigin(origins, `https://${env.REPLIT_DEV_DOMAIN.trim()}`);
  }
  if (env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
    for (const origin of [
      "http://127.0.0.1",
      "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
      "http://127.0.0.1:8080",
      "http://localhost",
      "http://localhost:5173",
      "http://localhost:8080",
    ]) {
      addExactOrigin(origins, origin);
    }
  }

  return origins;
}

export function trustedOriginGuard(
  trustedOrigins: ReadonlySet<string>,
): RequestHandler {
  return (req, res, next): void => {
    const origin = req.get("origin");
    if (origin && !trustedOrigins.has(origin)) {
      res.status(403).json({ error: "origin_not_allowed" });
      return;
    }
    next();
  };
}

export function trustedCorsOptions(
  trustedOrigins: ReadonlySet<string>,
): CorsOptions {
  return {
    credentials: true,
    origin(origin, callback) {
      callback(null, !origin || trustedOrigins.has(origin));
    },
  };
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/documentProfile.test.ts ================

import { describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import {
  documentSenderSource,
  missingDocumentProfileFields,
  senderSnapshotForCapture,
  senderSnapshotProfile,
  senderSnapshotValues,
  type DocumentProfile,
} from "./documentProfile";

const profile: DocumentProfile = {
  companyName: "Test Hyvleri AB", logoPath: "/objects/uploads/logo",
  streetAddress: "Testgatan 1", postalCode: "123 45", city: "Teststad", country: "Sverige",
  phone: "010-123", email: "test@example.se", organizationNumber: "556000-0000",
  vatNumber: "SE556000000001", website: "https://example.se",
  certificationText: "PEFC", certificationNumber: "PEFC/00-00-00", invoiceDetails: "Referens 1",
};

describe("document sender snapshots", () => {
  test("round-trips a complete immutable sender profile", () => {
    expect(senderSnapshotProfile(senderSnapshotValues(profile), { ...profile, companyName: "Ny profil" })).toEqual(profile);
  });

  test("uses current branding for legacy rows without a snapshot", () => {
    expect(senderSnapshotProfile({ senderCompanyName: null }, profile)).toEqual(profile);
  });

  test("keeps the first spec-confirmation snapshot after branding changes and reconfirmation", () => {
    const firstCapture = senderSnapshotForCapture({ senderCompanyName: null }, profile);
    expect(firstCapture).not.toBeNull();

    const changedBranding = { ...profile, companyName: "Nya Hyvleriet AB", city: "Ny stad" };
    const secondCapture = senderSnapshotForCapture(firstCapture!, changedBranding);
    expect(secondCapture).toBeNull();
    expect(senderSnapshotProfile(firstCapture!, changedBranding)).toMatchObject({
      companyName: "Test Hyvleri AB",
      city: "Teststad",
    });
  });

  test("captures an explicit Lego alternate sender without changing standard branding", () => {
    const alternate = { ...profile, companyName: "Lego Kund AB", email: "lego@example.se" };
    const capturedLegoSender = senderSnapshotForCapture(
      { senderCompanyName: null },
      documentSenderSource(alternate, profile),
    );
    expect(senderSnapshotProfile(capturedLegoSender!, profile)).toMatchObject({
      companyName: "Lego Kund AB",
      email: "lego@example.se",
    });
    // A later ordinary Lego job has no alternate sender and gets current standard branding.
    expect(documentSenderSource(null, profile)).toBe(profile);
    expect(profile.companyName).toBe("Test Hyvleri AB");
  });

  test("rejects an empty sender profile before a document is issued", () => {
    expect(missingDocumentProfileFields({
      ...profile,
      companyName: "",
      streetAddress: "",
      postalCode: "",
      city: "",
      country: "",
      phone: "",
      email: "",
    })).toEqual([
      "företagsnamn",
      "adress",
      "postnummer",
      "ort",
      "land",
      "telefonnummer",
      "e-postadress",
    ]);
  });

  test("keeps the exact captured logo path after later logo changes or removal", () => {
    const captured = senderSnapshotForCapture({ senderCompanyName: null }, profile);
    expect(captured?.senderLogoPath).toBe("/objects/uploads/logo");
    expect(senderSnapshotProfile(captured!, { ...profile, logoPath: "/objects/uploads/new-logo" }).logoPath)
      .toBe("/objects/uploads/logo");
    expect(senderSnapshotProfile(captured!, { ...profile, logoPath: null }).logoPath)
      .toBe("/objects/uploads/logo");
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/documentProfile.ts ================

import { brandingSettingsTable } from "@workspace/db/schema";

/** The sender identity printed on a document, independent of its recipient. */
export type DocumentProfile = {
  companyName: string;
  logoPath: string | null;
  streetAddress: string;
  postalCode: string;
  city: string;
  country: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string;
  organizationNumber: string | null;
  vatNumber: string | null;
  website: string | null;
  certificationText: string | null;
  certificationNumber: string | null;
  invoiceDetails: string | null;
  approvedForFTax: boolean;
};

type SnapshotSource = Record<`sender${string}`, string | boolean | null>;
export type SenderSnapshotValues = {
  [K in keyof DocumentProfile as `sender${Capitalize<K>}`]: DocumentProfile[K];
};

const profileFields = [
  "companyName", "logoPath", "streetAddress", "postalCode", "city", "country",
  "phone", "email", "organizationNumber", "vatNumber", "website",
  "certificationText", "certificationNumber", "invoiceDetails", "approvedForFTax",
] as const satisfies readonly (keyof DocumentProfile)[];

export function brandingToDocumentProfile(row: typeof brandingSettingsTable.$inferSelect): DocumentProfile {
  return Object.fromEntries(profileFields.map((field) => [field, row[field]])) as DocumentProfile;
}

export function senderSnapshotValues(profile: DocumentProfile): SenderSnapshotValues {
  return Object.fromEntries(profileFields.map((field) => [
    `sender${field[0]!.toUpperCase()}${field.slice(1)}`,
    profile[field],
  ])) as SenderSnapshotValues;
}

/**
 * Produces a write payload only for a document that has never been captured.
 * Callers still use a `sender_company_name IS NULL` predicate for concurrent
 * delivery/confirmation requests.
 */
export function senderSnapshotForCapture(
  existing: Partial<SnapshotSource>,
  source: DocumentProfile,
): SenderSnapshotValues | null {
  return hasSenderSnapshot(existing) ? null : senderSnapshotValues(source);
}

/** Lego's explicit alternate sender wins only for that Lego document. */
export function documentSenderSource(
  alternateSender: DocumentProfile | null,
  branding: DocumentProfile,
): DocumentProfile {
  return alternateSender ?? branding;
}

export function missingDocumentProfileFields(profile: DocumentProfile): string[] {
  const required = [
    ["companyName", "företagsnamn"],
    ["streetAddress", "adress"],
    ["postalCode", "postnummer"],
    ["city", "ort"],
    ["country", "land"],
    ["phone", "telefonnummer"],
    ["email", "e-postadress"],
  ] as const;
  return required
    .filter(([field]) => !profile[field]?.trim())
    .map(([, label]) => label);
}

export function hasSenderSnapshot(row: Partial<SnapshotSource>): boolean {
  return typeof row.senderCompanyName === "string";
}

/** Legacy orders without a snapshot deliberately resolve to current branding. */
export function senderSnapshotProfile(row: Partial<SnapshotSource>, fallback: DocumentProfile): DocumentProfile {
  if (!hasSenderSnapshot(row)) return fallback;
  return Object.fromEntries(profileFields.map((field) => {
    const key = `sender${field[0]!.toUpperCase()}${field.slice(1)}` as keyof SnapshotSource;
    return [field, row[key]];
  })) as DocumentProfile;
}

export function profileResponse(profile: DocumentProfile) {
  const logoUrl = profile.logoPath
    ? profile.logoPath.startsWith("/objects/")
      ? `/api/storage${profile.logoPath}`
      : profile.logoPath
    : null;
  return { ...profile, logoUrl };
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/impTillFuruMigration.ts ================

import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db, lagerHistorikTable, lagerPosterTable } from "@workspace/db";

export const IMP_TILL_FURU_ACTION = "historisk_imp_till_furu_v1";
export const IMP_TILL_FURU_CONFIRMATION = "KORRIGERA IMP TILL FURU";

export type ImpMigrationRecord = {
  id: number;
  pktNr: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  behandling: string | null;
  raderadAt: string | null;
};

export type ImpMigrationResult = {
  status: "ready" | "completed" | "already_completed";
  enabled: boolean;
  confirmationPhrase: string;
  wouldChange: number;
  changed: number;
  skipped: number;
  errors: number;
  records: ImpMigrationRecord[];
  executedAt: string | null;
};

const impPredicate = sql`LOWER(BTRIM(COALESCE(${lagerPosterTable.traslag}, ''))) = 'imp'`;

function mapRecord(row: typeof lagerPosterTable.$inferSelect): ImpMigrationRecord {
  const toNumber = (value: string | null) => value == null ? null : Number(value);
  return {
    id: row.id,
    pktNr: row.pktNr,
    profil: row.profil,
    nomTjocklek: toNumber(row.nomTjocklek),
    nomBredd: toNumber(row.nomBredd),
    behandling: row.behandling,
    raderadAt: row.raderadAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
  };
}

export async function dryRunImpTillFuru(
  action = IMP_TILL_FURU_ACTION,
): Promise<ImpMigrationResult> {
  const [marker] = await db
    .select()
    .from(lagerHistorikTable)
    .where(eq(lagerHistorikTable.action, action))
    .orderBy(lagerHistorikTable.id)
    .limit(1);
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(impPredicate)
    .orderBy(lagerPosterTable.id);

  return {
    status: marker ? "completed" : "ready",
    enabled: !marker,
    confirmationPhrase: IMP_TILL_FURU_CONFIRMATION,
    wouldChange: rows.length,
    changed: 0,
    skipped: 0,
    errors: 0,
    records: rows.map(mapRecord),
    executedAt: marker?.skapadAt.toISOString() ?? null,
  };
}

export async function executeImpTillFuru(
  changedBy: string,
  confirmation: string,
  action = IMP_TILL_FURU_ACTION,
): Promise<ImpMigrationResult> {
  if (confirmation !== IMP_TILL_FURU_CONFIRMATION) {
    throw new Error("confirmation_mismatch");
  }

  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    await tx.execute(sql`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${action}))`);

    const [marker] = await tx
      .select()
      .from(lagerHistorikTable)
      .where(eq(lagerHistorikTable.action, action))
      .limit(1)
      .for("update");

    if (marker) {
      return {
        status: "already_completed",
        enabled: false,
        confirmationPhrase: IMP_TILL_FURU_CONFIRMATION,
        wouldChange: 0,
        changed: 0,
        skipped: 0,
        errors: 0,
        records: [],
        executedAt: marker.skapadAt.toISOString(),
      };
    }

    const rows = await tx
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(impPredicate)
      .orderBy(lagerPosterTable.id)
      .for("update");
    const records = rows.map(mapRecord);

    if (rows.length === 0) {
      return {
        status: "ready",
        enabled: true,
        confirmationPhrase: IMP_TILL_FURU_CONFIRMATION,
        wouldChange: 0,
        changed: 0,
        skipped: 0,
        errors: 0,
        records: [],
        executedAt: null,
      };
    }

    const updated = await tx
      .update(lagerPosterTable)
      .set({
        traslag: "Furu",
        behandling: sql`CASE
          WHEN ${lagerPosterTable.behandling} IS NULL
            OR BTRIM(${lagerPosterTable.behandling}) = ''
          THEN 'Impregnerad'
          ELSE ${lagerPosterTable.behandling}
        END`,
      })
      .where(impPredicate)
      .returning({ id: lagerPosterTable.id });

    if (updated.length !== rows.length) {
      throw new Error("migration_row_count_mismatch");
    }

    await tx.insert(lagerHistorikTable).values(
      rows.map((row) => ({
        lagerId: row.id,
        action: `${action}:row`,
        changedBy,
        andringar: JSON.stringify({
          fore: { traslag: row.traslag, behandling: row.behandling },
          efter: {
            traslag: "Furu",
            behandling:
              row.behandling == null || row.behandling.trim() === ""
                ? "Impregnerad"
                : row.behandling,
          },
        }),
      })),
    );

    const [completion] = await tx
      .insert(lagerHistorikTable)
      .values({
        lagerId: rows[0].id,
        action,
        changedBy,
        andringar: JSON.stringify({
          status: "completed",
          changed: rows.length,
          skipped: 0,
          errors: 0,
          records,
        }),
      })
      .returning();

    return {
      status: "completed",
      enabled: false,
      confirmationPhrase: IMP_TILL_FURU_CONFIRMATION,
      wouldChange: 0,
      changed: updated.length,
      skipped: 0,
      errors: 0,
      records,
      executedAt: completion.skapadAt.toISOString(),
    };
  });
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/initialAdmin.test.ts ================

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from "vitest";

const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  execute: vi.fn(),
  insert: vi.fn(),
  values: vi.fn(),
  onConflictDoUpdate: vi.fn(),
  info: vi.fn(),
}));

vi.mock("@workspace/db", async (importOriginal) => {
  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import("@workspace/db")>();
  return {
    ...actual,
    db: {
      execute: mocks.execute,
      insert: mocks.insert,
    },
  };
});

vi.mock("./logger", () => ({
  logger: { info: mocks.info },
}));

import { seedInitialAdmin } from "./initialAdmin";

describe("INITIAL_ADMIN_EMAIL bootstrap", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    mocks.insert.mockReturnValue({ values: mocks.values });
    mocks.values.mockReturnValue({ onConflictDoUpdate: mocks.onConflictDoUpdate });
    mocks.onConflictDoUpdate.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
  });

  test("gör ingenting utan giltig konfiguration", async () => {
    await seedInitialAdmin(undefined);
    await seedInitialAdmin("inte-en-epost");

    expect(mocks.execute).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(mocks.insert).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test("normaliserar och upsertar den konfigurerade administratören", async () => {
    await seedInitialAdmin("  ADMIN@Example.COM ");

    expect(mocks.execute).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(mocks.values).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      email: "admin@example.com",
      rol: "admin",
      lagtTillAv: "system-seed",
    });
    expect(mocks.onConflictDoUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ set: { rol: "admin" } }),
    );
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/initialAdmin.ts ================

import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { tillatnaEmailsTable } from "@workspace/db/schema";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "./logger";

export async function seedInitialAdmin(
  rawEmail = process.env["INITIAL_ADMIN_EMAIL"],
): Promise<void> {
  const email = rawEmail?.toLowerCase().trim();
  if (!email || !email.includes("@")) return;

  await db.execute(sql`
    ALTER TABLE tillatna_emails ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS rol TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'anvandare'
  `);
  await db
    .insert(tillatnaEmailsTable)
    .values({ email, rol: "admin", lagtTillAv: "system-seed" })
    .onConflictDoUpdate({
      target: tillatnaEmailsTable.email,
      set: { rol: "admin" },
    });
  logger.info({ email }, "Initial admin seeded in allowlist");
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/lagerFilters.ts ================

import { isNull, ne, or, and, eq, ilike, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import type { SQL } from "drizzle-orm";
import { lagerPosterTable, lagerFackTable, db } from "@workspace/db";
import { normaliseraPaketBehandling } from "./paketBehandling";
import { normaliseraPaketUtforande } from "./paketUtforande";

export const BEHANDLING_AVRAPP_TYP = "hos_behandling";
export const LEGACY_IMPREGNERING_AVRAPP_TYP = "hos_impregnerare";

export function arHosBehandling(avrappTyp: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  return avrappTyp === BEHANDLING_AVRAPP_TYP
    || avrappTyp === LEGACY_IMPREGNERING_AVRAPP_TYP;
}

/**
 * De regler som avgör om en lagerpost räknas som "aktuellt lager" (i_lager),
 * utöver den exkludering av spec-importerade/mjukraderade poster som redan
 * gäller alla statusar i `GET /lager` (se routes/lager.ts). Detta är den
 * del av i_lager-villkoret som är specifik för statusen.
 */
export function iLagerExtraConditions(): SQL[] {
  return [
    // Endast poster som inte är utlevererade.
    isNull(lagerPosterTable.datumUt),
    // Paket hos behandling (inklusive den äldre impregneringsmarkören) är
    // tekniskt kvar men inte tillgängliga.
    or(isNull(lagerPosterTable.avrappTyp), ne(lagerPosterTable.avrappTyp, BEHANDLING_AVRAPP_TYP))!,
    or(isNull(lagerPosterTable.avrappTyp), ne(lagerPosterTable.avrappTyp, LEGACY_IMPREGNERING_AVRAPP_TYP))!,
  ];
}

/**
 * Fullständiga villkor för "aktuellt lager" (i_lager), inklusive den
 * spec_import/mjukraderad-exkludering som `GET /lager` applicerar för alla
 * statusar. Detta MÅSTE vara exakt samma regler som Lagerplatser-sidan och
 * `GET /lager?status=i_lager` använder, så att områdes-/fackstatistik
 * alltid matchar den befintliga vyn.
 */
export function iLagerConditions(): SQL[] {
  return [
    or(isNull(lagerPosterTable.avrappTyp), ne(lagerPosterTable.avrappTyp, "spec_import"))!,
    isNull(lagerPosterTable.raderadAt),
    ...iLagerExtraConditions(),
  ];
}

export function iLagerWhere(): SQL {
  return and(...iLagerConditions())!;
}

/**
 * Säkerställer att ett fack faktiskt hör till det angivna området, så att
 * en lagerpost aldrig kan kopplas till ett fack i ett annat område än
 * postens eget område. Kastar om kopplingen är ogiltig.
 */
export async function assertFackBelongsToOmrade(fackId: number | null | undefined, omradeId: number | null | undefined): Promise<void> {
  if (fackId == null) return;
  const [fack] = await db.select().from(lagerFackTable).where(eq(lagerFackTable.id, fackId));
  if (!fack) {
    throw new Error(`Fack ${fackId} finns inte`);
  }
  if (omradeId == null || fack.omradeId !== omradeId) {
    throw new Error(`Fack ${fackId} hör inte till område ${omradeId ?? "(inget)"}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Samtliga AKTUELLA (iLagerConditions) lager_poster-rader för ett givet
 * pktNr — ett fysiskt paket kan ligga som flera rader (en per längd), se
 * Task #18. Delas mellan `GET /lager/paket/:pktNr` och
 * inventeringsflödets skanning/klassificering (routes/inventering.ts) så
 * att båda alltid använder exakt samma definition av "vad ligger i paketet
 * just nu".
 */
export async function hamtaAktuellaPaketRader(pktNr: string) {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(and(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNr), ...iLagerConditions()));
}

/**
 * Fritextsök-villkor (whitespace = AND mellan termer) — EXAKT samma
 * matchningsregler som `GET /lager?search=` (pkt-nr, träslag, behandling, utförande,
 * profil, kvalitet, varuslag, plats, fritextfält, dimensioner, inkl.
 * slash-expansion som "28/29x100"). Delas med paketsökningen i den
 * aktiva inventeringsskanningen (routes/inventering.ts) så att båda
 * alltid söker likadant — se `.agents/memory/lager-inventering-paketsok.md`.
 */
export function buildLagerSearchConditions(search: string): SQL[] {
  const conditions: SQL[] = [];

  type SlashExpansion =
    | { type: "none" }
    | { type: "dim"; pairs: string[] }       // "28/29x100" → ["28x100","29x100"]
    | { type: "tjocklek"; values: string[] }; // "28/29" → ["28","29"]

  function expandSlash(term: string): SlashExpansion {
    const norm = term.replace(/[×]/g, "x");
    const dimM = norm.match(/^([\d.,]+(?:\/[\d.,]+)*)[xX]([\d.,]+(?:\/[\d.,]+)*)$/);
    if (dimM) {
      const ts = dimM[1].split("/");
      const bs = dimM[2].split("/");
      if (ts.length > 1 || bs.length > 1) {
        const pairs: string[] = [];
        for (const t of ts) for (const b of bs) pairs.push(`${t}x${b}`);
        return { type: "dim", pairs };
      }
      return { type: "none" };
    }
    const bareM = norm.match(/^[\d.,]+(\/[\d.,]+)+$/);
    if (bareM) return { type: "tjocklek", values: norm.split("/") };
    return { type: "none" };
  }

  const terms = search.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  for (const term of terms) {
    const expansion = expandSlash(term);

    if (expansion.type === "dim") {
      const dimOrs = expansion.pairs.map(dimStr => {
        const ds = dimStr.replace(/[×x]/gi, "x");
        const sub = `%${ds}%`;
        return or(
          sql`(CAST(${lagerPosterTable.nomTjocklek} AS TEXT) || 'x' || CAST(${lagerPosterTable.nomBredd} AS TEXT)) ILIKE ${sub}`,
          sql`(CAST(${lagerPosterTable.actTjocklek} AS TEXT) || 'x' || CAST(${lagerPosterTable.actBredd} AS TEXT)) ILIKE ${sub}`
        )!;
      });
      conditions.push(or(...dimOrs)!);
      continue;
    }

    if (expansion.type === "tjocklek") {
      const tOrs = expansion.values.map(v => or(
        sql`CAST(${lagerPosterTable.nomTjocklek} AS TEXT) ILIKE ${`%${v}%`}`,
        sql`CAST(${lagerPosterTable.actTjocklek} AS TEXT) ILIKE ${`%${v}%`}`
      )!);
      conditions.push(or(...tOrs)!);
      continue;
    }

    const t = term.replace(/[×x]/gi, "x");
    const sub = `%${t}%`;
    const normalizedTreatment = normaliseraPaketBehandling(t);
    const normalizedTreatmentSub = normalizedTreatment
      ? `%${normalizedTreatment}%`
      : sub;
    const normalizedExecution = normaliseraPaketUtforande(t);
    const normalizedExecutionSub = normalizedExecution
      ? `%${normalizedExecution}%`
      : sub;
    const exact = t;
    const wordLike = (col: any) => or(
      ilike(col, exact),
      ilike(col, `${t} %`),
      ilike(col, `% ${t}`),
      ilike(col, `% ${t} %`),
    )!;
    conditions.push(
      or(
        ilike(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, sub),
        ilike(lagerPosterTable.traslag, exact),
        sql`LOWER(${lagerPosterTable.traslag}) LIKE LOWER(${`${t}/%`})`,
        sql`LOWER(${lagerPosterTable.traslag}) LIKE LOWER(${`%/${t}`})`,
        or(
          ilike(lagerPosterTable.behandling, sub),
          ilike(lagerPosterTable.behandling, normalizedTreatmentSub),
        ),
        or(
          ilike(lagerPosterTable.utforande, sub),
          ilike(lagerPosterTable.utforande, normalizedExecutionSub),
        ),
        or(
          ilike(lagerPosterTable.profil, sub),
          sql`(${lagerPosterTable.profil} IS NULL AND 'sågat' ILIKE ${sub})`
        ),
        ilike(lagerPosterTable.kvalitet, sub),
        ilike(lagerPosterTable.varuslag, sub),
        wordLike(lagerPosterTable.plats),
        ilike(lagerPosterTable.produktion, sub),
        ilike(lagerPosterTable.anteckning, sub),
        ilike(lagerPosterTable.anmarkNing, sub),
        ilike(lagerPosterTable.mottaget, sub),
        sql`CAST(${lagerPosterTable.nomTjocklek} AS TEXT) ILIKE ${sub}`,
        sql`CAST(${lagerPosterTable.nomBredd} AS TEXT) ILIKE ${sub}`,
        sql`CAST(${lagerPosterTable.langdDm} AS TEXT) ILIKE ${sub}`,
        sql`(CAST(${lagerPosterTable.nomTjocklek} AS TEXT) || 'x' || CAST(${lagerPosterTable.nomBredd} AS TEXT)) ILIKE ${sub}`,
        sql`(CAST(${lagerPosterTable.actTjocklek} AS TEXT) || 'x' || CAST(${lagerPosterTable.actBredd} AS TEXT)) ILIKE ${sub}`
      )!
    );
  }

  return conditions;
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/objectStorage.ts ================

import { randomUUID } from "crypto";
import { Readable } from "stream";
import { Storage, type File } from "@google-cloud/storage";

const REPLIT_SIDECAR_ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1:1106";

export const objectStorageClient = new Storage({
  credentials: {
    audience: "replit",
    subject_token_type: "access_token",
    token_url: `${REPLIT_SIDECAR_ENDPOINT}/token`,
    type: "external_account",
    credential_source: {
      url: `${REPLIT_SIDECAR_ENDPOINT}/credential`,
      format: { type: "json", subject_token_field_name: "access_token" },
    },
    universe_domain: "googleapis.com",
  },
  projectId: "",
});

export class ObjectNotFoundError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super("Object not found");
    this.name = "ObjectNotFoundError";
  }
}

export class ObjectStorageService {
  private getPrivateObjectDir() {
    const dir = process.env.PRIVATE_OBJECT_DIR?.trim();
    if (!dir) throw new Error("PRIVATE_OBJECT_DIR is not configured");
    return dir.replace(/\/+$/, "");
  }

  async getObjectEntityUploadURL() {
    const { bucketName, objectName } = parseObjectPath(
      `${this.getPrivateObjectDir()}/uploads/${randomUUID()}`,
    );
    const response = await fetch(`${REPLIT_SIDECAR_ENDPOINT}/object-storage/signed-object-url`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        bucket_name: bucketName,
        object_name: objectName,
        method: "PUT",
        expires_at: new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
      }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to sign object URL: ${response.status}`);
    const body = (await response.json()) as { signed_url?: string };
    if (!body.signed_url) throw new Error("Object storage did not return a signed URL");
    return body.signed_url;
  }

  normalizeObjectEntityPath(rawPath: string) {
    if (!rawPath.startsWith("https://storage.googleapis.com/")) return rawPath;
    const objectDir = `${this.getPrivateObjectDir()}/`;
    const rawObjectPath = new URL(rawPath).pathname;
    return rawObjectPath.startsWith(objectDir)
      ? `/objects/${rawObjectPath.slice(objectDir.length)}`
      : rawObjectPath;
  }

  async getObjectEntityFile(objectPath: string): Promise<File> {
    if (!objectPath.startsWith("/objects/")) throw new ObjectNotFoundError();
    const { bucketName, objectName } = parseObjectPath(
      `${this.getPrivateObjectDir()}/${objectPath.slice("/objects/".length)}`,
    );
    const file = objectStorageClient.bucket(bucketName).file(objectName);
    const [exists] = await file.exists();
    if (!exists) throw new ObjectNotFoundError();
    return file;
  }

  async downloadObject(file: File) {
    const [metadata] = await file.getMetadata();
    const stream = Readable.toWeb(file.createReadStream()) as ReadableStream<Uint8Array>;
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      "Content-Type": metadata.contentType || "application/octet-stream",
      "Cache-Control": "private, max-age=3600",
    };
    if (metadata.size) headers["Content-Length"] = String(metadata.size);
    return new Response(stream, { headers });
  }
}

function parseObjectPath(path: string) {
  const normalized = path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`;
  const parts = normalized.split("/");
  if (parts.length < 3 || !parts[1] || !parts.slice(2).join("/")) {
    throw new Error("Invalid object storage path");
  }
  return { bucketName: parts[1], objectName: parts.slice(2).join("/") };
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/paketBehandling.ts ================

/**
 * Normaliserar uttryckliga behandlingsalias från både manuella formulär och
 * Paketlapp. Träslag och behandling hålls alltid som separata fält.
 */
export function normaliseraPaketBehandling(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (value == null) return null;

  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (trimmed === "") return null;

  const normalized = trimmed.toLocaleLowerCase("sv-SE");
  if (normalized === "imp" || normalized === "impregnerad") {
    return "Impregnerad";
  }
  if (
    normalized === "grundmålat" ||
    normalized === "grundmålad"
  ) {
    return "Grundmålad";
  }

  return trimmed;
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/paketUtforande.ts ================

/**
 * Normaliserar utförande från både manuella formulär och Paketlapp.
 * Utförande hålls alltid separat från profil, träslag, kvalitet och behandling.
 */
export function normaliseraPaketUtforande(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (value == null) return null;

  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (trimmed === "") return null;

  const normalized = trimmed.toLocaleLowerCase("sv-SE");
  if (/^4\s*rh$/.test(normalized)) return "4RH";
  if (normalized === "planhyvlat") return "Planhyvlat";
  if (normalized === "buntat") return "Buntat";

  return trimmed;
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/paketnummer.ts ================

export function normaliseraPaketnummer(
  pktNr: string | null | undefined,
): string | null {
  const normalized = pktNr?.trim();
  return normalized ? normalized : null;
}

export function paketnummerLasnyckel(pktNr: string): string {
  return `pkt:${pktNr.toLowerCase()}`;
}

export function sammaPaketnummer(
  left: string | null | undefined,
  right: string | null | undefined,
): boolean {
  const normalizedLeft = normaliseraPaketnummer(left);
  const normalizedRight = normaliseraPaketnummer(right);
  return normalizedLeft?.toLowerCase() === normalizedRight?.toLowerCase();
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/scanClassification.ts ================

type ScannedPackageClassification = {
  profil?: unknown;
  kvalitet?: unknown;
  anteckning?: unknown;
  nomTjocklek?: unknown;
  nomBredd?: unknown;
  aktTjocklek?: unknown;
  aktBredd?: unknown;
};

const C24_TOKEN = /(^|[^A-Z0-9])C24([^A-Z0-9]|$)/i;
const REGEL_TOKEN = /(^|[^A-ZÅÄÖ0-9])REGEL([^A-ZÅÄÖ0-9]|$)/i;
const RH_TOKEN = /4\s*(?:\/\s*4\s*)?RH/gi;

function asTrimmedString(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function hasToken(value: string, token: RegExp): boolean {
  token.lastIndex = 0;
  return token.test(value);
}

function hasRh(value: string): boolean {
  RH_TOKEN.lastIndex = 0;
  return RH_TOKEN.test(value);
}

function appendCanonicalRh(value: unknown): string {
  const note = asTrimmedString(value);
  if (!note) return "4 RH";
  if (hasRh(note)) {
    RH_TOKEN.lastIndex = 0;
    return note.replace(RH_TOKEN, "4 RH");
  }
  return `${note}, 4 RH`;
}

function isFiniteNumber(value: unknown): value is number {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value);
}

function hasFinishedDimensions(pkg: ScannedPackageClassification): boolean {
  return (
    pkg.nomTjocklek === 47 &&
    pkg.aktTjocklek === 45 &&
    isFiniteNumber(pkg.nomBredd) &&
    isFiniteNumber(pkg.aktBredd)
  );
}

/**
 * Normaliserar enbart tydliga C24-regelfall från följesedelsimporten.
 * Oklara C24/KD- och C24/4-fall lämnas orörda.
 */
export function normalizeScannedPackageClassification<T extends ScannedPackageClassification>(pkg: T): T {
  const profil = asTrimmedString(pkg.profil);
  const kvalitet = asTrimmedString(pkg.kvalitet);
  const anteckning = asTrimmedString(pkg.anteckning);

  const profilHarC24 = hasToken(profil, C24_TOKEN);
  const profilHarRegel = hasToken(profil, REGEL_TOKEN);
  const kvalitetHarC24 = hasToken(kvalitet, C24_TOKEN);
  const rhFinns = hasRh(profil) || hasRh(kvalitet) || hasRh(anteckning);
  const kvalitetArTom = kvalitet.length === 0;
  const kvalitetArEndastRh = hasRh(kvalitet) && !kvalitetHarC24;
  const kvalitetArC24EllerC24Rh = kvalitetHarC24;

  const c24OchRegelFramgar =
    (profilHarRegel && (profilHarC24 || kvalitetHarC24)) ||
    (profil.toLowerCase() === "regel" && kvalitetHarC24);

  const felplaceradC24MedFardigmatt =
    profil.toLowerCase() === "c24" &&
    hasFinishedDimensions(pkg) &&
    (kvalitetArTom || kvalitetArEndastRh || kvalitetArC24EllerC24Rh);

  if (!c24OchRegelFramgar && !felplaceradC24MedFardigmatt) return pkg;

  pkg.profil = "Regel";
  pkg.kvalitet = "C24";
  if (rhFinns) pkg.anteckning = appendCanonicalRh(pkg.anteckning);
  return pkg;
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/lib/structuredLagerplats.ts ================

import { db, lagerFackTable, lagerOmradenTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";

export async function resolveStructuredPlatsPatch(
  patch: Record<string, unknown>,
): Promise<{ ok: true; patch: Record<string, unknown> } | { ok: false; error: string }> {
  const hasOmradeField = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(patch, "omradeId");
  const hasFackField = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(patch, "fackId");

  if (!hasOmradeField && hasFackField) {
    return { ok: false, error: "fackId kräver omradeId" };
  }

  if (!hasOmradeField) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(patch, "plats")) {
      return { ok: true, patch: { ...patch, omradeId: null, fackId: null } };
    }
    return { ok: true, patch };
  }

  const omradeIdRaw = patch.omradeId;
  if (omradeIdRaw === null) {
    return { ok: true, patch: { ...patch, omradeId: null, fackId: null } };
  }

  const omradeId = Number(omradeIdRaw);
  if (!Number.isInteger(omradeId)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "Ogiltigt omradeId" };
  }

  const [omrade] = await db
    .select()
    .from(lagerOmradenTable)
    .where(and(eq(lagerOmradenTable.id, omradeId), eq(lagerOmradenTable.aktiv, true)));
  if (!omrade) {
    return { ok: false, error: "Området hittades inte eller är inaktivt" };
  }

  let fackId: number | null = null;
  if (hasFackField && patch.fackId !== null && patch.fackId !== undefined) {
    const fackIdNum = Number(patch.fackId);
    if (!Number.isInteger(fackIdNum)) {
      return { ok: false, error: "Ogiltigt fackId" };
    }
    const [fack] = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerFackTable)
      .where(and(eq(lagerFackTable.id, fackIdNum), eq(lagerFackTable.aktiv, true)));
    if (!fack) {
      return { ok: false, error: "Facket hittades inte eller är inaktivt" };
    }
    if (fack.omradeId !== omradeId) {
      return { ok: false, error: "Valt fack tillhör inte valt område" };
    }
    fackId = fackIdNum;
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    patch: { ...patch, plats: omrade.namn, omradeId, fackId },
  };
}

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/middlewares/clerkProxyMiddleware.ts ================

/**
 * Clerk Frontend API Proxy Middleware
 *
 * Proxies Clerk Frontend API requests through your domain, enabling Clerk
 * authentication on custom domains and .replit.app deployments without
 * requiring CNAME DNS configuration.
 *
 * AUTH CONFIGURATION: To manage users, enable/disable login providers
 * (Google, GitHub, etc.), change app branding, or configure OAuth credentials,
 * use the Auth pane in the workspace toolbar. There is no external Clerk
 * dashboard — all auth configuration is done through the Auth pane.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * - Only active in production (Clerk proxying doesn't work for dev instances)
 * - Must be mounted BEFORE express.json() middleware
 *
 * Usage in app.ts:
 *   import { CLERK_PROXY_PATH, clerkProxyMiddleware } from "./middlewares/clerkProxyMiddleware";
 *   app.use(CLERK_PROXY_PATH, clerkProxyMiddleware());
 */

import { createProxyMiddleware } from "http-proxy-middleware";
import type { RequestHandler } from "express";

const CLERK_FAPI = "https://frontend-api.clerk.dev";
export const CLERK_PROXY_PATH = "/api/__clerk";

export function clerkProxyMiddleware(): RequestHandler {
  // Only run proxy in production — Clerk proxying doesn't work for dev instances
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
    return (_req, _res, next) => next();
  }

  const secretKey = process.env.CLERK_SECRET_KEY;
  if (!secretKey) {
    return (_req, _res, next) => next();
  }

  return createProxyMiddleware({
    target: CLERK_FAPI,
    changeOrigin: true,
    pathRewrite: (path: string) =>
      path.replace(new RegExp(`^${CLERK_PROXY_PATH}`), ""),
    on: {
      proxyReq: (proxyReq, req) => {
        const protocol = req.headers["x-forwarded-proto"] || "https";
        const host = req.headers.host || "";
        const proxyUrl = `${protocol}://${host}${CLERK_PROXY_PATH}`;

        proxyReq.setHeader("Clerk-Proxy-Url", proxyUrl);
        proxyReq.setHeader("Clerk-Secret-Key", secretKey);

        const xff = req.headers["x-forwarded-for"];
        const clientIp =
          (Array.isArray(xff) ? xff[0] : xff)?.split(",")[0]?.trim() ||
          req.socket?.remoteAddress ||
          "";
        if (clientIp) {
          proxyReq.setHeader("X-Forwarded-For", clientIp);
        }
      },
    },
  }) as RequestHandler;
}
